                                setTimeout(function() {
                                    $('fieldset.product-form__input[data-opcount="2"]').find('label[data-title="' + op22 + '"]').trigger('click');
                                }, 1000);
                            }
                            ;if (op33 && $('fieldset.product-form__input[data-opcount="3"]').length > 0) {
                                setTimeout(function() {
                                    $('fieldset.product-form__input[data-opcount="3"]').find('label[data-title="' + op33 + '"]').trigger('click');
                                }, 1000);
                            }
                            ;if (getCookie('optionPIDlist')) {
                                setCookie('option1select', op11, 7);
                                setCookie('option2select', op22, 7);
                                setCookie('option3select', op33, 7);
                            }
                        }
                        // added by anusha - 4th variant option cookie approach - end

                        if (vat_free_day == true) {
                            var vat_price_footer = $(".pricestyle").find(".vat_price_bold").text();
                            if (vat_price_footer != "") {
                                $("#price-element").find(".footerOr2").css("display", "none");
                            }
                        }
                    });
                </script>
                <script>
                    $(document).on('change', 'input[type="number"]', function(event) {
                        this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]+/g, '');
                        if (this.value < 1)
                            this.value = 0;
                    });
                    $(document).on('keypress', 'input[type="number"]', function(event) {
                        if ((event.target.value.length == 0 && event.which == 48) || (event.target.value.length == 0 && event.which > 31 && event.which < 48) || (event.target.value.length == 0 && event.which > 57 && event.which < 128)) {
                            return false;
                        }
                    });
                    $(document).on('keypress', 'input[type="number"]', function(event) {
                        return (((event.which > 47) && (event.which < 58)) || (event.which == 13));
                    });

                    // reload page on back button click since the cached version on Safari browsers is incorrect
                    $(window).bind("pageshow", function(event) {
                        if (event.originalEvent.persisted) {
                            window.location.reload();
                        }
                    });

                    function updateBodyPadding() {
                        // update body padding to avoid content hidden behind the sticky footer
                        const footerHeight = document.querySelector('.js-sticky-atc')?.offsetHeight || 0;
                        document.body.style.paddingBottom = `${footerHeight}px`;
                    }

                    updateBodyPadding();
                    window.addEventListener('resize', updateBodyPadding);
                </script>
                <script>
                    $(document).ready(function() {
                        const mainSKUElement = document.querySelector('#main-pro-sku');
                        const initialSKUValue = mainSKUElement?.value;

                        function init() {
                            initAllPickupAvailabilityElements();
                            initAllShippingAvailabilityElements();

                            if (!window.ctoCollection) {
                                const $defaultOption = $('#bundleOptionNo');
                                $defaultOption.click();
                                updateAllShippingAvailabilityElements(window.shippingMessage);
                                updateAllPickupAvailabilityElements(window.pickupMessageType);
                            }

                            addEvents();
                        }

                        function addEvents() {
                            $(document).on('change', '#bundleForm', function(event) {
                                initAllPickupAvailabilityElements();
                                initAllShippingAvailabilityElements();
                                updateActiveBundleOption();
                                updateAllCTOPriceDisplays();
                            });
                            $(document).on('click', '#apple-care-add', function(event) {
                                updateAllCTOPriceDisplays();
                            });
                            $(document).on('click', '#secWarranty-add', function(event) {
                                updateAllCTOPriceDisplays();
                            });
                            document.addEventListener('bundle:optionQtyInCartUpdated', function() {
                                updateActiveBundleOption();
                            });
                        }

                        function initAllPickupAvailabilityElements() {
                            window.pickupAvailabilityElements = document.querySelectorAll('pickup-availability');
                            window.pickupMessageType = document.querySelector('.cto-bundle-wrapper')?.getAttribute('data-no-bundle-type');
                            window.storeLocatorElement = document.querySelector('my-store-locator');
                        }

                        function initAllShippingAvailabilityElements() {
                            window.shippingAvailabilityElements = document.querySelectorAll('.apl-section-shipping__message-wrapper');
                            window.shippingMessage = document.querySelector('.cto-bundle-wrapper')?.getAttribute('data-no-bundle-shipping-message');
                        }

                        function updateAllPickupAvailabilityElements(messageType) {
                            if (window.pickupAvailabilityElements?.length && window.storeLocatorElement && messageType) {
                                const allLocationsData = window.storeLocatorElement.allLocationsData;
                                const preferredStoreId = window.storeLocatorElement.preferredStoreId;

                                window.pickupAvailabilityElements?.forEach( (pickup) => {
                                    pickup.setAttribute('data-message-type', messageType);
                                    pickup.renderPreview(allLocationsData?.find( (location) => location.id === preferredStoreId));
                                }
                                );
                            }
                        }

                        function updateAllShippingAvailabilityElements(message) {
                            if (window.shippingAvailabilityElements?.length && message) {
                                window.shippingAvailabilityElements.forEach( (elem) => {
                                    elem.innerHTML = message;
                                }
                                );
                            }
                        }

                        function updateActiveBundleOption() {
                            const $selectedOption = $('input[name="bundle_option"]:checked');
                            const $productQuantityWrapper = $('.product-form__quantity .quantity__rules-cart');
                            const $productQuantityInCartElem = $('.product-form__quantity .quantity-cart');
                            const $productQuantityInCart = parseInt($productQuantityInCartElem?.attr('data-qty-in-cart-default') || '0', 10);
                            const $bundleQuantityInCart = parseInt($selectedOption.attr('data-bundles-qty-already-in-cart') || '0', 10);
                            // selected option in the configurable accordion
                            $('.js-cto-bundle-selected-option').html($selectedOption.val());

                            // selected option in the summary section
                            $('.js-cto-summary-bundle-value').html($selectedOption.val());
                            $('.js-cto-summary-bundle').removeClass('hidden');

                            if ($selectedOption.attr('id') !== 'bundleOptionNo' && !$selectedOption.attr('data-bundle-name')) {
                                mainSKUElement.value = $selectedOption.attr('data-bundle-sku');
                            } else {
                                mainSKUElement.value = initialSKUValue;
                            }

                            if ($selectedOption.attr('id') !== 'bundleOptionNo') {
                                $productQuantityInCartElem?.html($bundleQuantityInCart);
                                $productQuantityWrapper?.toggleClass('hidden', $bundleQuantityInCart === 0);
                            } else {
                                $productQuantityInCartElem?.html($productQuantityInCart);
                                $productQuantityWrapper?.toggleClass('hidden', $productQuantityInCart === 0);
                            }

                            const storeLocator = document.querySelector('my-store-locator');
                            const storeLocatorDrawer = document.querySelector('my-store-locator-drawer');
                            const allLocationsData = storeLocator.allLocationsData;
                            const searchLocation = storeLocator.storeLocatorSearch.value;

                            if (storeLocatorDrawer && allLocationsData) {
                                storeLocatorDrawer.updateStoreDetails(allLocationsData, searchLocation);
                            }

                            const bundleMaxLimit = $selectedOption.attr('data-max');
                            const productQuantityElement = document.querySelector('#prodQuantity');
                            const productQuantityElementPlusBtn = document.querySelector('.js-qty-input button[name="plus"]');
                            const productMaxQuantityElement = document.querySelector('#product_max_quantity');

                            if (bundleMaxLimit) {
                                if (productQuantityElement) {
                                    productQuantityElement.setAttribute('data-max', bundleMaxLimit);
                                    productQuantityElement.setAttribute('max', bundleMaxLimit);
                                }

                                if (productQuantityElement) {
                                    productMaxQuantityElement.value = bundleMaxLimit;
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (productQuantityElement) {
                                    productQuantityElement.removeAttribute('data-max');
                                    productQuantityElement.removeAttribute('max');
                                    productQuantityElementPlusBtn?.classList.remove('disabled');
                                }

                                if (productQuantityElement) {
                                    productMaxQuantityElement.value = '/';
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        function updateAllCTOPriceDisplays() {
                            let defaultProductPrice = parseInt(document.querySelector('.js-product-price-with-care-warranty[data-price-raw]')?.dataset.priceRaw) || 0;
                            let defaultProductCompareAtPrice = parseInt(document.querySelector('.js-product-compare-at-price[data-compare-at-price-raw]')?.dataset.compareAtPriceRaw) || 0;
                            const discountType = document.querySelector('.product')?.getAttribute('data-discount-type');

                            // If a bundle is selected, override the default product price and compare at price
                            const $selectedBundleOption = document.querySelector('input.js-cto-set-price-display:checked');
                            const bundleShippingMessage = document.querySelector('.cto-bundle-wrapper')?.getAttribute('data-bundle-shipping-message');

                            if ($selectedBundleOption) {
                                defaultProductPrice = parseInt($selectedBundleOption.dataset.priceRawSetAs) || 0;
                                defaultProductCompareAtPrice = parseInt($selectedBundleOption.dataset.compareAtPriceRawSetAs) || 0;
                                updateAllShippingAvailabilityElements(bundleShippingMessage);
                                updateAllPickupAvailabilityElements('cto');
                            } else {
                                updateAllShippingAvailabilityElements(window.shippingMessage);

                                if (window.pickupMessageType) {
                                    updateAllPickupAvailabilityElements(window.pickupMessageType);
                                } else {
                                    // No-bundle type is empty (e.g. unavailable product) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reset pickup to initial state
                                    const allLocationsData = window.storeLocatorElement?.allLocationsData;
                                    const preferredStoreId = window.storeLocatorElement?.preferredStoreId;
                                    window.pickupAvailabilityElements?.forEach( (pickup) => {
                                        pickup.setAttribute('data-message-type', '');
                                        pickup.renderPreview(allLocationsData?.find( (location) => location.id === preferredStoreId));
                                    }
                                    );
                                }
                            }

                            // All input elements that have a class of "js-cto-modify-price-display" and are checked will modify the price calculation by their [data-price-raw-modify-by] attribute
                            const addOnsPrices = [...document.querySelectorAll('input.js-modify-price-display:checked')].map( (el) => parseInt(el.dataset.priceRawModifyBy) || 0);
                            const addOnPriceTotal = addOnsPrices.reduce( (acc, price) => acc + price, 0);

                            // trade-in currently excluded from price
                            const tradeInIncludedInPrice = false;
                            const tradeInPrice = tradeInIncludedInPrice ? (parseFloat(document.getElementById('selected-trade-in-value')?.value) || 0) * -100 : 0;

                            const sumOfAllPrices = defaultProductPrice + addOnsPrices.reduce( (acc, price) => acc + price, 0) + tradeInPrice;
                            const sumOfAllPricesWithoutAddOns = defaultProductPrice + tradeInPrice;
                            const sumOfAllCompareAtPrices = defaultProductCompareAtPrice + tradeInPrice;

                            const priceCalculation = parseInt(document.querySelector('.js-product-price-with-care-warranty[data-price-calculation]')?.dataset.priceCalculation) || 1;
                            const truncateTo2Decimals = (num) => {
                                return Math.floor(num * 100) / 100;
                            }
                            ;

                            const discountPercent = sumOfAllCompareAtPrices > sumOfAllPricesWithoutAddOns ? sumOfAllCompareAtPrices > 0 ? ((1 - sumOfAllPricesWithoutAddOns / sumOfAllCompareAtPrices) * 100).toFixed(2).replace('.00', '') : 0 : 0;

                            const discountMoneyValue = sumOfAllCompareAtPrices > sumOfAllPricesWithoutAddOns ? sumOfAllCompareAtPrices > 0 ? sumOfAllCompareAtPrices - sumOfAllPricesWithoutAddOns.toFixed(2) : 0 : 0;

                            const showCompareAtPrice = +discountPercent > 0 || +discountMoneyValue > 0;

                            // final formatted prices
                            const formattedTotalPrice = formatPriceWithCurrency(sumOfAllPrices / 100);
                            const formattedFromTotalPrice = SDG.Data.productPriceFrom.replace(`\{\{ price \}\}`, formattedTotalPrice);
                            const formattedNmpPrice = formatPriceWithoutCurrency(truncateTo2Decimals(sumOfAllPrices / priceCalculation / 100));
                            const formattedTotalCompareAtPrice = formatPriceWithCurrency(sumOfAllCompareAtPrices / 100);
                            const formattedDiscountPercent = `-${discountPercent}%`;
                            const formattedDiscountMoneyValue = `-${formatPriceWithoutCurrency(discountMoneyValue / 100)}`;
                            const $perMonthPrice = document.querySelectorAll('.js-nmp-price') || [];
                            let nmpBasePrice = null;

                            const $priceDisplays = document.querySelectorAll('.js-product-price-with-care-warranty');
                            $priceDisplays.forEach( ($priceDisplay) => {
                                // do not update price with VAT priceRawSetAs
                                if ($priceDisplay.classList.contains('js-product-price-with-care-warranty-vat')) {
                                    const regularPrice = parseInt($priceDisplay.dataset?.priceRaw) || 0;
                                    const sumOfRegularPrice = regularPrice + addOnsPrices.reduce( (acc, price) => acc + price, 0) + tradeInPrice;
                                    const priceWithVAT = formatPriceWithCurrency(sumOfRegularPrice / 100);
                                    const formattedNmpWithVAT = formatPriceWithoutCurrency(truncateTo2Decimals(sumOfRegularPrice / priceCalculation / 100));

                                    if (priceWithVAT) {
                                        $priceDisplay.innerHTML = priceWithVAT;
                                        // use regular price for per month calculations
                                        nmpBasePrice = formattedNmpWithVAT;

                                        // default to bundle prices if bundle is selected
                                        if ($selectedBundleOption) {
                                            $priceDisplay.innerHTML = formattedTotalPrice;
                                            nmpBasePrice = formattedNmpPrice;
                                        }
                                        return;
                                    }
                                }

                                const $fromPrice = $priceDisplay.querySelector('.js-cto-from-pricing');
                                const $normalPrice = $priceDisplay.querySelector('.js-cto-dynamic-pricing');
                                if ($fromPrice || $normalPrice) {
                                    if ($fromPrice) {
                                        $fromPrice.innerHTML = formattedFromTotalPrice;
                                    }
                                    if ($normalPrice) {
                                        $normalPrice.innerHTML = formattedTotalPrice;
                                    }
                                } else if ($priceDisplay.closest('.net-monthly-pricing, .js-net-monthly-pricing-pdp-trigger')) {
                                    $priceDisplay.innerHTML = formattedNmpPrice;
                                } else {
                                    $priceDisplay.innerHTML = formattedTotalPrice;

                                    if ($selectedBundleOption) {
                                        const vatBundlePrice = parseInt($selectedBundleOption.dataset.vatPriceRawSetAs) || 0;

                                        if (vatBundlePrice > 0) {
                                            const sumOfVatRegularPrice = vatBundlePrice + addOnsPrices.reduce( (acc, price) => acc + price, 0) + tradeInPrice;
                                            const priceWithVATBundle = formatPriceWithCurrency(sumOfVatRegularPrice / 100);
                                            $priceDisplay.innerHTML = priceWithVATBundle;
                                        } else {
                                            // if no VAT price is set, use the regular price
                                            $priceDisplay.innerHTML = formattedTotalPrice;
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            );

                            // update per month price displays
                            if ($perMonthPrice.length > 0) {
                                $perMonthPrice.forEach( ($perMonth) => {
                                    $perMonth.innerHTML = nmpBasePrice || formattedNmpPrice;
                                }
                                );
                            }

                            const $compareAtPriceDisplays = document.querySelectorAll('.js-product-compare-at-price');
                            $compareAtPriceDisplays.forEach( ($compareAtPriceDisplay) => {
                                if (showCompareAtPrice) {
                                    const $compareAtPrice = $compareAtPriceDisplay.querySelector('.js-product-compare-at-price-slash');
                                    const $discountPercent = $compareAtPriceDisplay.querySelector('.js-product-compare-at-price-percent');
                                    $compareAtPrice.innerHTML = formattedTotalCompareAtPrice;
                                    if (discountType === 'currency') {
                                        $discountPercent.innerHTML = formattedDiscountMoneyValue;
                                    } else {
                                        $discountPercent.innerHTML = formattedDiscountPercent;
                                    }
                                    $compareAtPriceDisplay.classList.remove('hidden');
                                } else {
                                    $compareAtPriceDisplay.classList.add('hidden');
                                }
                            }
                            );
                        }

                        init();
                    });
                </script>

                <link rel="stylesheet" href="//www.digimap.co.id/cdn/shop/t/36/assets/product-scratch.css?v=148507075148348439441777963976" media="print" onload="this.media='all'">
                <div class="frequent-products" data-section-id="template--20033597800599__frequently-bought-products">
                    <product-recommendations class="product-recommendations1 page-width section-template--20033597800599__frequently-bought-products-padding isolate apl-section-frequently-bought-products" data-url="/recommendations/products?section_id=template--20033597800599__frequently-bought-products&product_id=9183584714903&limit=3">
                        <div class="product-recommendations__cta hidden" style="height:0px;">

                                &nbsp;<span class="js-frequent-items-text-check" data-single-item-message="Item to Cart" data-multiple-items-message="Items to Cart">Item to Cart
        </span>
                                <div class="loading-overlay__spinner-frequent hidden">
                                    <svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" role="presentation" class="spinner" viewBox="0 0 66 66" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

                <script>
                    const labelForCheck = `<span class="selected">selected</span>
      <span class="unselected">not selected</span>`;
                    class ProductRecommendations extends HTMLElement {
                        constructor() {
                            super();
                            this.config = {
                                dom: {
                                    button: '.js-frequent-submit',
                                    input: '.js-frequent-products-checkbox:not(.enabled)',
                                    selected: 'input[name="frequent-products-pdp"]:checked',
                                    button_txt: '.js-frequent-items-text-check',
                                },
                                cls: {
                                    hide: 'hide',
                                    enabled: 'enabled',
                                },
                                labelForCheck,
                            };
                            const handleIntersection = (entries, observer) => {
                                if (!entries[0].isIntersecting)
                                    return;
                                observer.unobserve(this);
                                fetch(this.dataset.url).then( (response) => response.text()).then( (text) => {
                                    const html = document.createElement('div');
                                    html.innerHTML = text;
                                    const recommendations = html.querySelector('product-recommendations');
                                    if (recommendations && recommendations.innerHTML.trim().length) {
                                        this.innerHTML = recommendations.innerHTML;
                                        const $sliderWrap = this.querySelector('.product-list-contain-frequent');
                                        if ($sliderWrap) {
                                            // re-add events when product variant updates
                                            const handleMutation = (mutationList, observer) => {
                                                for (const mutation of mutationList) {
                                                    this.addEvent();
                                                }

                                                setTimeout( () => {
                                                    var height;
                                                    var maxheight = 0;
                                                    var minheight = 0;
                                                    $('.price_container_cal').each(function() {
                                                        height = parseInt($(this).height());

                                                        if (height > maxheight) {
                                                            maxheight = height;
                                                        } else {
                                                            minheight = height;
                                                        }
                                                    });
                                                    let isMobile = window.matchMedia('only screen and (max-width: 1023px)').matches;
                                                    if (!isMobile) {
                                                        if (maxheight >= minheight) {
                                                            $('.price_container_cal').attr('style', 'display: flex; flex-flow: column; height:' + maxheight + 'px;');
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                                , 500)
                                            }
                                            ;
                                            const mutationConfig = {
                                                attributes: false,
                                                childList: true,
                                                subtree: true
                                            };
                                            const mutationObserver = new MutationObserver(handleMutation.bind(this));
                                            mutationObserver.observe($sliderWrap, mutationConfig);
                                        }
                                    }
                                    if (html.querySelector('.grid__item')) {
                                        this.classList.add('product-recommendations--loaded');
                                    }

                                    this.addEvent();
                                    const $selected = this.querySelectorAll(this.config.dom.selected);
                                    this.updateButtonState($selected.length > 0);
                                    if ($selected.length > 0) {
                                        this.checkButtonText($selected.length);
                                    }
                                    const $checkedProducts = this.querySelector('#checked-products');
                                    if ($checkedProducts) {
                                        $checkedProducts.textContent = $selected.length;
                                    }
                                    call_gafunction();
                                }
                                ).catch( (e) => {
                                    console.error(e);
                                }
                                );
                            }
                            ;
                            new IntersectionObserver(handleIntersection.bind(this),{
                                rootMargin: '0px 0px 200px 0px'
                            }).observe(this);
                        }
                        hideButton(el) {
                            if (!el)
                                return;
                            // add style to force safari to repaint later
                            el.style.display = 'none';
                            el.classList.add(this.config.cls.hide);
                        }
                        showButton(el) {
                            if (!el)
                                return;
                            // remove style to force safari to repaint
                            el.removeAttribute('style');
                            el.classList.remove(this.config.cls.hide);
                        }
                        updateButtonState(show=true) {
                            const $button = this.querySelector(this.config.dom.button) || null;
                            if (!$button)
                                return;

                            if (show) {
                                this.showButton($button);
                            } else {
                                this.hideButton($button);
                            }
                        }
                        checkButtonText(number) {
                            const buttonTextElem = this.querySelector(this.config.dom.button_txt) || null;
                            const singleItemText = buttonTextElem?.getAttribute('data-single-item-message') || "";
                            const multipleItemsText = buttonTextElem?.getAttribute('data-multiple-items-message') || "";

                            if (number === 1) {
                                buttonTextElem.textContent = `${singleItemText}`;
                            } else {
                                buttonTextElem.textContent = `${multipleItemsText}`;
                            }
                        }
                        addEvent() {
                            const $inputs = this.querySelectorAll(this.config.dom.input) || [];
                            // bind modals
                            if (window?.refreshModals) {
                                window.refreshModals();
                            }

                            if (!$inputs?.length > 0)
                                return;
                            [...$inputs].forEach( ($input) => {
                                $input.classList.add(this.config.cls.enabled);
                                // set correct label for check
                                const $labelForCheck = $input.parentElement.querySelector('.label-for-check');
                                if ($labelForCheck) {
                                    $labelForCheck.innerHTML = this.config.labelForCheck;
                                }
                                $input.addEventListener('change', () => {
                                    const $selected = this.querySelector(this.config.dom.selected) || null;
                                    const selectedNumber = this.querySelectorAll(this.config.dom.selected).length || null;

                                    if ($selected) {
                                        this.updateButtonState();
                                        this.checkButtonText(selectedNumber);
                                    } else {
                                        this.updateButtonState(false);
                                    }
                                }
                                );
                            }
                            );
                        }
                    }
                    customElements.define('product-recommendations', ProductRecommendations);

                    function call_gafunction() {
                        var mpn_handle = $('#mpn_handle').val();
                        var frequentProducts_ga = [];

                            var compareprice = $('.card_product_compare_' + $(this).val()).attr('value');
                            prodPrice = Number(prodPrice.replace(/[^0-9.-]+/g, ''));
                            var discount = 0;
                            if (compareprice != '') {
                                compareprice = Number(compareprice.replace(/[^0-9.-]+/g, ''));
                                discount = parseFloat(compareprice) - parseFloat(prodPrice);
                                discount = parseFloat(discount);
                                // prodPrice = compareprice;
                            }
                            var productPrice1 = parseFloat(prodPrice);
                            item_ga = {};
                            item_ga['item_id'] = prodSku;
                            item_ga['item_name'] = prodtitle;
                            item_ga['item_category'] = prodType;
                            item_ga['item_list_name'] = productList;
                            item_ga['price'] = prodPrice;
                            item_ga['quantity'] = 1;
                            frequentProducts_ga.push(item_ga);
                        });
                        if (frequentProducts_ga.length != 0) {
                            dataLayer.push({
                                ecommerce: null
                            });
                            dataLayer.push({
                                event: 'view_item_list',
                                ecommerce: {
                                    item_list_name: productList,
                                    items: frequentProducts_ga,
                                },
                            });
                        }

    const observer=new MutationObserver(scheduleApply);observer.observe(root,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true});
    root.addEventListener('click',function(e){const a=e.target.closest('a');if(!a)return;const href=(a.getAttribute('href')||'').trim();if(/\/products\//i.test(href)||/\/cart/i.test(href)||/\/checkout/i.test(href))e.preventDefault();},true);
})();
</script>

                var acc = document.getElementsByClassName('accordion2');

                $(document).on('click', '.accordion2', function() {
                    this.classList.toggle('active_2');
                    this.classList.toggle('headerBlu');

], ['button[type="submit"]', ['align-self', 'center'], ['font-weight', '600'], ['padding-left', '50px'], ['padding-right', '50px'], ['margin-top', '34px'], ], ['button[type="submit"]:hover', ['background-color', 'var(--color-primary-bt-hover, #62bbfa)'], ['border-color', 'var(--color-primary-bt-hover)'], ['text-decoration', 'underline'], ['filter', 'opacity(1)', true], ], ['legend[class^="_textBody"]', ['font-size', '20px !important'], ['line-height', '20px'], ['font-weight', '500'], ['padding', '22px 0 6px'], ['letter-spacing', '0.4px'], ], ['a', ['text-decoration', 'none'], ], ['a:hover', ['color', '#0054A8'], ['text-decoration', 'underline'], ['text-underline-offset', '0.3rem'], ['text-decoration-thickness', '0.1rem'], ], ['[class^="_arrowDownIcon_"] svg', ['width', '10px', true], ['height', '6px', true], ['background-image', 'url(\'data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 10 6" width="10" height="6"%3E%3Cpath fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M9.354.646a.5.5 0 00-.708 0L5 4.293 1.354.646a.5.5 0 00-.708.708l4 4a.5.5 0 00.708 0l4-4a.5.5 0 000-.708z"                         const successObserver = new MutationObserver( (mList, obs) => {
                            const shadowForm = shadow.querySelector('#app-embed');
                            const hasSuccess = shadowForm?.dataset?.currentStep === 'success';
                            if (hasSuccess) {
                                obs.disconnect();
                                // place success message into modal and trigger modal open
                                const successMessage = shadowForm.querySelector('section[class^="_formBannerContainer_"]');
                                const modalContent = document.getElementById('modal-b2b-form-success');
                                const modalTrigger = document.querySelector('a[data-modal-target="modal-b2b-form-success"]');
                                if (successMessage && modalContent && modalTrigger) {
                                    const modalClose = modalContent.querySelector('.js-close-modal-primary');
                                    if (modalClose) {
                                        modalContent.insertBefore(successMessage, modalClose);
                                    } else {
                                        modalContent.appendChild(successMessage);
                                    }
                                    modalTrigger.click();
                                }

                                // adobe analytics tracking
                                Shopify.analytics.publish('APLdigitalDataEvent', {
                                    'event': 'form_submit',
                                    'form_name': 'B2B Registration Form',
                                });
                            }
                        }
                        );
                        successObserver.observe(shadow, {
                            childList: true,
                            subtree: true
                        });

                        const employeeInput = shadow.getElementById('custom#number_of_employees');
                        if (employeeInput) {
                            employeeInput.setAttribute('onkeydown', 'return event.keyCode !== 69 && event.keyCode !== 187 && event.keyCode !== 189 && event.keyCode !== 107 && event.keyCode !== 109 && event.keyCode !== 190 && event.keyCode !== 188');
                            employeeInput.setAttribute('min', 0);
                        }

                        /**
          * Add a stylesheet rule to the document (it may be better practice
          * to dynamically change classes, so style information can be kept in
          * genuine stylesheets and avoid adding extra elements to the DOM).
          * Note that an array is needed for declarations and rules since ECMAScript does
          * not guarantee a predictable object iteration order, and since CSS is
          * order-dependent.
          * @param {Array} rules Accepts an array of JSON-encoded declarations
          * @example
        addStylesheetRules([
          ['h2', // Also accepts a second argument as an array of arrays instead
            ['color', 'red'],
            ['background-color', 'green', true] // 'true' for !important rules
          ],
          ['.myClass',
            ['background-color', 'yellow']
          ]
        ]);
        */
                        function addStylesheetRules(rules) {
                            const sheet = new CSSStyleSheet();
                            for (let i = 0; i < rules.length; i++) {
                                let j = 1
                                  , rule = rules[i]
                                  , selector = rule[0]
                                  , propStr = "";
                                // If the second argument of a rule is an array of arrays, correct our variables.
                                if (Array.isArray(rule[1][0])) {
                                    rule = rule[1];
                                    j = 0;
                                }

                                for (let pl = rule.length; j < pl; j++) {
                                    const prop = rule[j];
                                    // If the second argument of a prop is an array, go deeper.
                                    if (Array.isArray(prop[1])) {
                                        const subprops = prop[1];
                                        let subpopStr = "";
                                        for (let k = 1; k < subprops.length; k++) {
                                            const subprop = prop[k];
                                            subpopStr += `${subprop[0]}: ${subprop[1]}${subprop[2] ? " !important" : ""};\n`;
                                        }
                                        propStr += `${prop[0]}{${subpopStr}}\n`;
                                    } else {
                                        propStr += `${prop[0]}: ${prop[1]}${prop[2] ? " !important" : ""};\n`;
                                    }
                                }
                                // Insert CSS Rule
                                sheet.insertRule(`${selector}{${propStr}}`, sheet.cssRules.length, );
                            }
                            shadow.adoptedStyleSheets.push(sheet);
                        }
                    }
                }
                );

                formObserver.observe(document.body, {
                    childList: true,
                    subtree: true
                });
            });
        </script>

            <script>
                document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
                    document.querySelector(".popup-close-button").addEventListener("click", function() {
                        document.querySelector("#option-dialog").classList.add("hidden");
                    });
                });

                async function fetchWithTimeout(resource, options={}) {
                    const {timeout=30000} = options;

                    const controller = new AbortController();
                    const id = setTimeout( () => controller.abort(), timeout);

                    try {
                        const response = await fetch(resource, {
                            ...options,
                            signal: controller.signal
                        });
                        return response;
                    } catch (error) {
                        if (error.name === 'AbortError') {
                            throw new Error('WeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢re having trouble connecting. It looks like the server is taking too long to respond. You can try refreshing the page or checking back in a few minutes');
                        }
                        throw error;
                    } finally {
                        clearTimeout(id);
                    }
                }
            </script>

<script id="nagaliga-single-hero-guard">
(function(){
  const hero = 'https://i.postimg.cc/Kc3HKqyj/nuvo77.webp';
  const heroAlt = 'NUVO77';
  let busy = false;

  function desktopMarkup(){
    return '<li id="Slide-template--20033597800599__main-nagaliga" class="product__media-item grid__item is-active" data-media-position="0">' +
      '<div class="product-media-container media-type-image" style="width:100%;">' +
      '<div class="product__media media media--transparent" style="padding-top:100%;position:relative;overflow:hidden;border-radius:20px;">' +
      '<img src="'+hero+'" alt="'+heroAlt+'" width="1254" height="1254" loading="eager" fetchpriority="high" style="position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;">' +
      '</div></div></li>';
  }
  function mobileMarkup(){
    return '<li data-media-position="0"><img src="'+hero+'" alt="'+heroAlt+'" width="1254" height="1254" loading="eager" fetchpriority="high" style="width:100%;height:auto;object-fit:cover;border-radius:18px;display:block;"></li>';
  }

  function enforce(){
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    try {
      const mobile = document.querySelector('.mobile_media_slider .slider_media');
      if (mobile) {
        if (window.jQuery && window.jQuery.fn && window.jQuery.fn.slick && mobile.classList.contains('slick-initialized')) {
          try { window.jQuery(mobile).slick('unslick'); } catch(e) {}
        }
        const ok = mobile.children.length === 1 && mobile.querySelector('img[src="'+hero+'"]') && !mobile.classList.contains('slick-initialized');
        if (!ok) mobile.innerHTML = mobileMarkup();
      }
      const desktop = document.getElementById('Slider-Gallery-template--20033597800599__main');
      if (desktop) {
        const ok = desktop.children.length === 1 && desktop.querySelector('img[src="'+hero+'"]');
        if (!ok) desktop.innerHTML = desktopMarkup();
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.product__media-wrapper .slider-buttons, .product__media-wrapper .product__media-icon, .product__media-wrapper .thumbnail-slider').forEach(el => el.style.display='none');
    } finally { busy = false; }
  }

  function start(){
    enforce();
    const wrap = document.querySelector('.product__media-wrapper');
    if (wrap) {
      const mo = new MutationObserver(() => requestAnimationFrame(enforce));
      mo.observe(wrap, {subtree:true, childList:true});
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('variant-radios1 label')) e.preventDefault();
    }, true);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start, {once:true});
  else start();
  window.addEventListener('load', enforce, {once:true});
})();
</script>
